# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-12)

## Corpus Check
- 5389 files · ~12,319,446 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 68054 nodes · 183675 edges · 3290 communities (1598 shown, 1692 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5961 edges (avg confidence: 0.74)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `b2527f4f`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
